# HANDOFF — status of the collection rollout

Short status page, updated with each train. The recipe and working rules live in `CLAUDE.md`, layout and manual rules in `tools/CONVENTIONS.md`, lever and vendor quirks in `tools/GOTCHAS.md`, the pipeline in `tools/README.md`, the subagent brief in `tools/CAPTURE_AGENT.md`. A new session needs nothing else: "continue with the NS SNG" is a complete prompt.

## Done (2026-10-10)

- Phase A tooling: `capture_train.js`, `draft_profile.js` (+ `families.json`), `draft_manual.js`, `readme_status.js` (+ `trains.json`), `crop_image.ps1`, `stitch_cars.ps1`, `tsw_input.ps1`. Verified: the drafter regenerates the Class 153 profile from its capture.
- Phase B: desktop computer-use tools never appeared in the session; `tools/tsw_input.ps1` (PowerShell screenshots + simulated input) drives the TSW menus reliably instead, and a capture subagent runs that loop (`tools/CAPTURE_AGENT.md`).
- NS SNG (2026-10-10): captured as `RVM_ZGN_NS_SNG3_Cab_mBk_C` (the 11:20 SPR service leads with the mBk car), profile `tsw-ns-sng-dtg-thejag-1791600993`, schematic manual with desk photo and Arthur's 3-car drawing, release built, cab-tested by the user, README `DONE ✅`. Combined power/brake handle continuous on the right TCA throttle (-0.9..1, 0 dead band raw 0.5 ±0.02 with fixed min-brake -0.1 and 5 %-power 0.1 notches beside it, free brake and power zones outside, Emergency left out; user decisions), left throttle unassigned, headlight intensity on the flap lever, parking brake apply/release on the left/right throttle buttons, slow-speed mode on the round aux, signal lights on AUTO BRK.
- DB BR 440 revisited (2026-10-10): real capture `tools/captures/RVM_NWB_DB_BR440_0_C.json` + handle probe, profile cleaned (direct controls only) with the SNG handle geometry (Emergency left out, 0 band ±0.02), manual with cab band, train brake pills in orientation order (Drive on top), start dot at Max brake; cab-tested by the user ("handles quite nicely"). No side view (user choice: Commons has no drawing of the Coradia Continental and the photo was too small); the renderer spreads the checklists and the cab band over the free space (`nodiag`).
- NS ICMm: recaptured (`tools/captures/RVM_ZGN_NS_ICMm3_mBfk_C.json`), profile cleaned (direct controls, ATB/deadman fuses on the aux squares, traction reduction on the left throttle button), new schematic manual with the desk photo band and Arthur's 3-car drawing, release rebuilt, legacy docx removed. README stays `DONE ✅`; the user still has to drive it with the new profile once (notch pill orientation: lever forward = raw 0, see CLAUDE.md).
- BR Class 153 redone (2026-10-10): real capture `tools/captures/RVM_TFW_Class153_PRM_C.json` (Cardiff City Network, 2C34 Penarth–Coryton, single car, cab pair L/S), profile `tsw-class-153-dtg-thejag-1791606571` drafted from it: train brake on the left TCA throttle and power handle on the right as in the Sprinter cab (desk photo: brake with its round base on the left, throttle slot on the right), throttle inverted (forward = 7), reverser 0..0.75 inverted (forward = Forward, back = Off), AWS reset stays key Q like the 333, sander as direct control. Manual with cab band, pills in orientation order (Forward / Release / 7 on top), start dots Off / Full service / Off, drawing credited to Vauxhallvauxhall (Commons CC BY-SA 4.0; Arthur's site has no British DMUs). First cab test (user): throttle and brake went the wrong way; profile fixed (throttle plain, brake inverted). Second check (user): the cab now works the right way (power pulled towards you, brake pushed away) and the manual pills must match the cab: Off / Emergency / Forward at the top (far end), 7 / Release / Off at the bottom (nearest the driver), which is the drafter's default from the profile. Lever direction rule written into CLAUDE.md (four steps). The ICMm, SNG and BR 440 manuals were right as drafted. Headlight dial dot is always on Off. README still `SOON 🟨`: second cab test pending.
- BR Class 333 revisited (2026-10-10): real capture `tools/captures/RVM_AWL_NT_Class333_DMSO_B_C.json` (Airedale & Wharfedale, 2H63 Skipton–Leeds, 4 cars, bare node names; the TE_/BE_ cab-pair detection was a false positive and was cleared by hand), profile `tsw-class-333-dtg-thejag-1791609075` drafted from it (same assignments as the tested one without the sync_control blocks; sander now a direct control, AWS reset stays key Q). Cab: handle Emergency at the far end, P4 nearest the driver (raw 0 = far, plain lever); reverser Off at the far end, Reverse nearest (raw 1 = far, inverted) — the tested profile already matched, only the manual's handle column was upside down. Manual with desk band, handle pills Emergency…P4, start dot on B3, Commons credit (Vauxhallvauxhall). README stays `DONE ✅`; the user should drive it once with the regenerated profile.
- BR Class 171 (Rivet) brought to the fleet standard (2026-10-10): real capture `tools/captures/RVM_BR_Class171_DMCL_W_C.json` (East Coastway, 1G29 Brighton–Ashford, 2-car 171/7; the handle's P1–P7 notches were swept by hand with the reverser in Forward because the script sweeps in Neutral where the handle stops at Off), profile `tsw-class-171-rivet-thejag-1791610728` regenerated from the user's own layout (family `uk-dmu-turbostar`): sync block dropped, horns and sander as direct controls, AWS reset / doors / wipers / cab light still game keys, headlight dial now Off / Day running / Marker / Night running / Tail only (the back-up variants were dropped for the Off default). Cab: brake at the far end, full power nearest the driver (plain lever, Emergency left out); reverser Off far, Reverse near (inverted). Schematic manual with desk band and WestRail642fan's 2-car drawing (Commons CC BY-SA 4.0), Southern + Rivet logos, docx removed. Cab test: P1, P4, P5 beeped on micro-movements; two causes found and fixed (step values a hair below the game's k/14 notch positions re-snapped on every re-send; TCA detents on band edges), see the CLAUDE.md gotcha; user confirmed fixed. README `DONE ✅`.

- BR Class 313 and 314 brought to the fleet standard (2026-10-10): captures `RVM_ECW_SN_Class313_DMSO_A_C` (East Coastway 2020, 2C24 Brighton–Seaford) and `RVM_GCC_ScR_Class314_DMSO_A_C` (Cathcart Circle 2019, 2N34 Glasgow Central–Neilston); the two PEP units share every node the profile uses with identical notch values (only brake notch 3 is "Full Service" on the 313 and "Step 3" on the 314), so the 314 profile is the 313 profile with the other class names. Layout from the uk-dmu template: brake on the left TCA throttle (inverted, Emergency far), power on the right (plain, Off far, 4 nearest the driver, checked with API screenshots of the handle), reverser like the 333 (inverted, Off far). AWS reset key Q, sander/horns/buzzer direct, AWS-TPWS and DSD isolation on the aux squares with the polarity of the old 2026-03 profiles (lit = value 0; an API test with the self-test showed nothing, the cab test must confirm which way is isolated), wipers relative 0.5 (Fast/Park/Slow), headlight dial Off/Marker/Head+marker/Tail/Off. Manuals with desk bands and Mangoslyce's 3-car Commons drawings (Southern Coastway, ScotRail saltire; Arthur's site has no British stock), ScotRail logo `tools/assets/logo-scotrail.svg` (already navy). Old profiles and docx removed. README stays `DONE ✅`; both still need one drive with the new profiles. Note: the 314 power controller only moves with the reverser in Forward (the capture script retried that itself).

- BR Class 375 (Firefly) and 377 (DTG) brought to the fleet standard (2026-10-10): captures `RVM_MVL_SE_Class375-6_DMCO_A_C` (Medway Valley Line, 2T44 Strood–Tonbridge) and `RVM_ECW_SN_Class377-4_DMCO_A_C` (East Coastway 2018, 2D33 Brighton–Ore). The 375 sweep left the handle's power side locked at Off (the script sweeps in Neutral); P1–P4 were probed by hand with the reverser in Forward (stored 0.25/0.5/0.75/1, one API set crosses one gate at a time as on the BR 440), the 377 sweep got them itself. Both Electrostars share every node the profile uses, so the 377 profile is the 375 one with the 377 class names (ECW 377-3/-4, LBN 377-4, EMK 377-2). Combined handle plain on the left TCA throttle (Emergency far, P4 nearest, POWER marked at the near end of the quadrant), reverser inverted (Off far), start dot B3. AWS reset key Q, sand/horns/guard buzzer direct (the 377 SignalBell node has no identifier but is the same push button: cab test), AWS and DSD+vigilance isolation on the aux squares (lit = Isolated = 0, like the 313/314), wipers game keys v / shift+v, headlight dial Off / Day / Marker / Night / Tail with the separate TailLights node. Manuals with desk bands and WestRail642fan's Commons drawings (375/6 4-car, 377/4 4-car; 375/3 and 377/3 drawings in tools/assets for later), Southeastern wordmark recoloured to navy, no public Firefly Simulations logo (named in the eyebrow only). New family `uk-electrostar` (375 template). Old profiles and docx removed; README stays `DONE ✅`, both need one drive.

- BR Class 43 HST (DTG, 2026-10-10): capture `tools/captures/RVM_EMT_Class43_C.json` (Midland Main Line, 1D21 St Pancras–Sheffield driven Leicester–Derby, EMT 2+8 set, no cab pair, engine off at load). Profile `tsw-class-43-hst-dtg-thejag-1791638637` from the uk-dmu template with the levers swapped to the HST desk: power controller on the left TCA throttle (plain: Idle far, Max nearest the driver, steps 0.2), train brake on the right (inverted, raw 0..7: Running nearest, Emergency far, start dot Full service), reverser -2..1 inverted (Engine off / Reverse / Neutral / Forward). Master key (ToggleButton) on PARK BRK, engine stop / start on the throttle buttons (no sander), horn ±1 with rest 0, AWS reset key Q, AWS_Isolation (no identifier) and DSD_Isolation on the aux squares (lit = Isolated = 0), guard buzzer round aux, wipers relative 1/3 (Off / Parked / On / Wash), headlight dial Off / Day / Markers / Night / Tail on the -2..2 SimpleLever. Notes: the PC locked itself during the first capture attempt (screen lock) and the user unlocked it; `pdf2png.ps1` needs an existing output folder and backslash paths.

- BR Class 66 (DTG, 2026-10-10): capture `tools/captures/RVM_AWL_DB_Class66__C.json` (Airedale & Wharfedale, 6M31 Hunslet East–Arcow Quarry part 4 from Kirkstall Loop, DB Cargo loco with 26 wagons; cab pair F/B by prefix and suffix, but `IS_GetActiveCab` answers "Node failed to return valid data" on this loco, so the profile is hard-wired to the F cab: if nothing moves in the cab test, swap every `_F` / `F_` node to B). Profile `tsw-class-66-dtg-thejag-1791639508` written by hand (the drafter only gave the skeleton): train brake F_AutoBrake on the left TCA throttle and loco brake F_DirectBrake on the flap lever, both Release / Hold / Apply (-1 / 0 / 1, inverted: Apply at the far end as on the desk labels; the sweep saw one notch because the output is a rate, not a position), throttle Throttle_F on the right (plain, Stop far, 8 near, the nine stored notch values 0 … 0.9514), reverser -1 / 0 / 1 inverted (Forward far). Reverser handle (MasterSwitch) on PARK BRK, sand stick toggle on the left throttle button, engine start on the right, horn 1 / 0 with rest 0.5, AWS reset key Q, parking brake apply / release on RUD TRIM, slow speed control toggle on the round aux, AWS/TPWS and DSD fuses on the aux squares (lit = Isolated = 0), wipers L+R relative 0.5, headlight dial Off / Day / Yard / Night / Tail (Day -0.5, Yard -1, Night 0.5 on the F lever; the B lever has Day and Night swapped; tail lights via F_TailLights). Rail classes: every Class66 in the pak index (AWL/EMK/PBD/SEH DB, EWS, DBS, ECW, CL packs, EFP DRS/ONE); only the AWL DB one was captured, the older EWS/DBS models may use other node names. No side view: Commons has only photos and Arthur has no UK livery (his HGK red drawing is in tools/assets, uncommitted, if the user prefers it); the page uses the no-diagram layout. DB mark from Commons recoloured to navy (`logo-db-cargo-navy.svg`). Desk photo retaken wider; the handles sit below the frame. README `SOON 🟨`: cab test pending.

- BR Class 86/4 (Just Trains, 2026-10-11): capture `tools/captures/RVM_JT_BR_Class86-4_C.json` (WCML Crewe–Preston, 1S61 Cardiff–Glasgow from Crewe, 86420 BR blue with 12 Mk coaches; nodes `_F`/`_B`, `IS_GetActiveCab` fails, F cab hard-wired). The script could not free the reverser, train brake and loco brake (the JT loco needs MasterKeySwitch_F = 1 and the brake key CabChangeLever_F = 1, which the script did not recognise), so Fable probed them by hand through the API: reverser Off 0.5 / Forward 1 (Reverse 0 only engages while ReverserLockButton_F is held, so the TCA can set it only with the lock pressed in the cab); train brake raw gates Running 0.175 (any set below 0.26), 1st application 0.35, service continuous 0.36–0.675, Full service 0.675, Emergency 0.84, Shutdown 1 (Release/overcharge 0.04 is not reachable by a set); loco brake continuous 0–1; power controller tap-changer Off 0 / Run down 0.2 / Notch down 0.4 / Hold 0.6 / Notch up 0.8 (Run up never came up). Profile `tsw-class-86-jt-thejag-1791641073`: train brake on the left TCA throttle (plain: Running far, Emergency nearest; service zone as a null step with its own band 0.29–0.76; Shutdown left out), power controller on the right (inverted: Off nearest, Notch up far, as the curved handle lies at Off towards the driver), loco brake continuous on the flap lever, reverser inverted (Forward far). PARK BRK sets master key and brake key together, pantograph up on the right throttle button, both sanders on the left, horn Horn_FL (raw 0 = high, 1 = low, rest 0.5), parking brake apply / release on RUD TRIM (spring lever 1 / 0, rest 0.5), guard buzzer round aux, AWS isolation (0.8078 = normal) and DSD breaker on the aux squares (lit = isolated), wipers L+R relative 0.34 (Park / Stop / Run), lights dial Off / Markers / Head + markers / Tail left / Tail right from the separate HeadLight, Marker and TailLightSwitch levers. Rail classes: every JT 86/4 and 86/2 variant (the 86/2 add-on is the same model, not captured). No drawing exists (Commons, Arthur, web), no-diagram layout; InterCity swallow (Commons CC BY-SA 4.0) recoloured to navy, Just Trains mark tinted navy from their own site. README `SOON 🟨`. Rotary handles: the brake and reverser directions are a guess from the photo, the cab test decides.

- BR Class 87 (Just Trains, 2026-10-11): capture `tools/captures/RVM_JT_BR_Class87_C.json` (WCML Preston–Carlisle, 1O11 Glasgow–Poole from Carlisle, BR blue). Same cab logic, node names and brake gates as the 86 (Fable re-probed them through the API with both keys in: Running 0.175, 1st application 0.35, service 0.36–0.675, Full service 0.675, Emergency 0.84, Shutdown 1; loco brake continuous; Reverse only with the lock button held), so the profile `tsw-class-87-jt-thejag-1791642429` is the 86 profile with the 87 class names; the only node difference is the marker lights (Marker_FL and Marker_FR instead of Marker_F), both set together on the lights dial. Manual copied from the 86 with the Preston–Carlisle desk photo. No drawing exists; InterCity + Just Trains logos. README `SOON 🟨`. The capture script now ignores driving levers when it looks for keys (the JT reverser is named MasterSwitch_F), but it still does not set the JT brake key itself: identifier BrakeKey matches, so it should from now on, to be confirmed on the next JT loco.

- BR Class 90 (Alan Thomson Simulation, 2026-10-11): capture `tools/captures/RVM_ATS_Class90_DB_Blue_C.json` (the loco add-on runs on WCML South, timetable "Class 90 Freight Weekday", 4M63 Felixstowe–Garston from Willesden, DB blue, 26 wagons; nodes `_F`/`_B`, IS_GetActiveCab false/false, F cab hard-wired). Profile `tsw-class-90-ats-thejag-1791644055`: train brake on the left TCA throttle (plain, stored notch values 0.1 / 0.22 / 0.35 / 0.5 / 0.65 / 0.8 / 0.9 / 1, Running far, Emergency nearest, start dot Full service), power handle on the right (plain: Off 0 ±0.04, Min 0.1 band 0.07–0.17, continuous zone 0.19–0.955 mapped to 0.11..0.99, Max 1 ±0.035), rheostatic brake DynamicBrake_F on the flap lever (same stored values 0.1 … 0.9, Release far), reverser 0 / 0.33 / 0.66 / 1 inverted (Forward far). The straight air brake DirectBrake_F is left out: a set above 0.5 springs back to Hold within 400 ms, only Release (0) sticks. Master key push button on PARK BRK, sand left button, right button raises the pan AND pulses the main circuit breaker to Close (press again once the pan is up), horn 1 / 0 rest 0.5, parking brake on / off push buttons on RUD TRIM, DRA toggle on the round aux (no buzzer on a freight loco), AWS isolation (Isolated = 1 on this loco) and DSD isolation (Isolated = 0) on the aux squares, wipers L+R relative 0.34, lights dial from HeadLightSwitch (Day 0 / Off 0.5 / Night 1) + MarkerLights + TailLights. Rail classes: all ATS 90 liveries (DB blue / red, Freightliner green new / old, grey, C90F_FKA). No drawing exists; DB mark; no ATS logo (name in the eyebrow only). README `SOON 🟨`.

- DB BR 612 (DTG, 2026-10-11): capture `tools/captures/RVM_DCZ_DB_BR612_A_C.json` (Tharandter Rampe, IRE1 3087 Nürnberg–Dresden driven from Chemnitz Hbf 12:01, 2-car unit; one node set, no cab suffixes, `IS_GetActiveCab` false/false, `GetActiveCab` fails). The combined handle `ThrottleAndBrakeHandle` is a custom class (`BP_DCZ_DB_BR612_ThrottleAndBrakeHandle_C`) that `capture_train.js` skips, so it was probed by hand through the API: seven notches at inputs 0 / 0.15 / 0.3 / 0.5 / 0.7 / 0.85 / 1 = Brake + / Brake hold / Brake − / Off / Throttle − / Throttle hold / Throttle + (outputs -3..3, `GetCurrentNotchIndex` 0..6), the game moves it ONE notch per API set (as the BR 440 / 700 handles), and the two ends are spring positions: a set to 0 or 1 pulses the internal `Property.ApplyAmount` (about ±0.007 per set, `ApplySensitivity` 0.067 per second held) and the stored input falls back to the hold notch (0.15 / 0.85). ApplyAmount is read-only and `bIsTractionLocked` stayed true in the probe (transmission switched on, reverser Forwards, brake Full service, doors open at the platform), so the traction side could not be exercised. `TrainBrakeHandle` eleven notches 0 / 0.2 / 0.275 / 0.35 / 0.425 / 0.5 / 0.575 / 0.65 / 0.725 / 0.8 / 1; `Reverser` Backwards 0 / OFF 0.5 / Forwards 1; `ShuntingControlL/R` (window shunting levers, identifier Throttle) not assigned; no AFB; keys `ReverserKey` (MasterSwitch) / `BrakeKey` push buttons Inserted 1, both read 0 during the sweep and the levers moved anyway. Profile `tsw-br-612-dtg-thejag-1791726381` (`tools/mk/mk612.js`): handle inverted on the left TCA throttle with seven bands (Throttle + at raw 0..0.06, hold 0.07..0.21, Throttle − 0.24..0.40, Off 0.5 ±0.03, Brake − 0.60..0.76, hold 0.79..0.93, Brake + 0.94..1), Führerbremsventil plain on the right with the 171 bands, Richtungsschalter inverted (three steps), flap free; PARK BRK = ReverserKey then BrakeKey; SiFa reset key Q; SanderSwitch / HornSwitch (High) / PzbReleaseSwitch / PzbAcknowledgeSwitch / PzbOverrideSwitch spring levers 1 / rest 0.5; SifaFuseControl / PZBFuseControl push-button toggles 1 / 0 (GNTFuseControl stays in the cab); doors on `DoorsSideSelectionSwitch` (Unlock Left 0.75 / Unlock Right 0.25 while the RUD TRIM side is held, Lock All 0 on release); WiperSwitch relative 0.25 (Off 0 / Intermittent 0.25 / Slow 0.75 / Fast 1 as swept); lights dial SignalLightsSwitch Off 0.5 / Headlights 0.125 + HeadlightsSwitch Reduced 0 / Normal 0.5 / Brights 1 / Taillights 0.875. Rail classes BR612_A / _B. No drawing yet (search running); DB grey scheme, Chemnitz desk photo 2.87:1 (band position 50 % 60 %). README `SOON 🟨`. Cab test must decide the handle above all: whether holding the TCA lever at the far end keeps raising the traction demand (the app re-sends on every axis change, the game pulses once per set) or only nudges it; if it only nudges, the fallback is the game keys (A / D hold the handle at the + end, DigitalSensitivity 1) on momentary assignments, or the TSW "simple controls" option, which may turn the handle into a direct setpoint lever (unverified). Also: the handle and brake directions, the door selector polarity and whether the brake key must be in before the brake valve moves.

The 365 names the Fahrschalter notches: Idle 0 / Fill-Run down 0.33 / Hold 0.67 / Increasing 1 (spring, never stores): the V 60 throttle is an incremental hydraulic handle, so the 363 profile and manual were corrected the same day (four bands: Increase at the far end, Hold, Run down, Idle; the old "Stufe 1 / 2" labels were wrong). `DriversBrakeValve_L` Release/Running 0.25 / Neutral 0.41 / Lap 0.57 / Service Application 0.73 / Emergency 1 (Overcharge below never stores), `IndependentBrakeValve_L` Release -1 / Hold 0 / Apply spring, `MasterKey` lever 0 / 1, `GearSelector` 0 / 0.656. Profile `tsw-br-365-dtg-thejag-1791729033` (`tools/mk/mk365.js`, derived from mk363 by text replacement): otherwise the 363 layout (left desk, Throttle_L / DriversBrakeValve_L / IndependentBrakeValve_L on the flap, MasterKey on PARK BRK, Fuse_Sifa / PZB_Breaker toggles, six Headlight switches on the dial, doors y / u); wipers are off the controller. Rail class RVM_NID_DBB_BR365_C. Drawing: DB Einsatzmerkblatt BR 345–365 (05/2008) via diagrams.json; Bad Vilbel desk photo 2.77:1. Store id 4679400 (Niddertalbahn, as the 628). README `SOON 🟨`. Cab test must decide: whether S / W really step the reverser in the game (else find the keys in the TSW bindings), the incremental throttle (does holding the TCA at Increase keep raising the demand), the brake bands, and whether to drive from the right desk.

- DB BR 194 / E 94 088 (DTG, BR 194 & E94 Railtour Pack, 2026-10-12): capture `tools/captures/RVM_DBB_BR194_C.json` (Linke Rheinstrecke, timetable Koblenz–Mainz 1984, freight 43877 Mainz–Koblenz 11:32 from Mainz Hbf, 31 wagons; two cabs `_F` / `_B`, GetActiveCab false/false, F cab wired). Three interlocks found by API probe: (1) the Führerbremsventil `TrainBrake_F` only moves with `BrakeKey_F` at 0 (Unlocked; the loco loads with it at 1 = Locked, which is why the sweep saw one notch), one gate per set, notches Release 0.2 / Neutral 0.32 / Lap 0.435 / Service 0.6 / Emergency 1 (0.8 and up), Overcharge below Release is a spring and never stores; (2) the Richtungsschalter `MasterSwitch_F` moves Off ↔ Forward freely but only at Fahrstufe 0 (it was stuck at Forward while the tap changer stood at Stufe 8), and Reverse needs `ReverserLockButton_F` at 1; (3) the Fahrschalter `PowerController_F (E94Controller)` (class OBB_1020_PowerController_C, not matched by the capture script) is a spring handwheel, rest 0.5, whose Fahrstufe is `Property.TertiaryValue` 0..18 (read-only): it winds only with the reverser engaged, a set to 1 / 0 pulses about 0.26 Stufe and falls back, re-sends every 100 ms give about 3.6 Stufen a second, and `Interacting=1` plus a held value winds continuously. `LocoBrakeValve_F` Release 0 / Hold 0.5, Apply is a spring that never stored. SiFa switches through the `Sifa_StateSetter` push button (Normal 1 / Isolated 0; it drives `SifaIsolatingValve`, `Sifa_MCB` and the Störschalter), PZB through `Indusi_Schalter` (On 1 / Off 0); both load off. Profile `tsw-br-194-dtg-thejag-1791734003` (`tools/mk/mk194.js`): MasterSwitch_F inverted on the speedbrake; PowerController_F on the left TCA throttle as three bands (Wind up raw 0 ±0.25 / Hold 0.5 ±0.2 / Wind down 1 ±0.25, inverted) and on the mode switch as held momentary sets (left 0, right 1, release 0.5); TrainBrake_F plain on the right with the 363 bands; LocoBrakeValve_F on the flap; PARK BRK = BrakeKey_F 0 / 1 plus ReverserLockButton_F 1 / 0; SiFa key Q; Sanders_F 0.5 / 0; Whistle_F 1 / 0; Indusi_*_F spring levers 1 / 0.5; Sifa_StateSetter / Indusi_Schalter toggles; doors y / u; lights dial from LightingSwitch_F (Off 0 / Half 0.5 / Full 1), the six SignalLight levers and TailLampRear. Wiper (`Wiper_F` 0..1 continuous speed) and MotorBrakeSwitch_F (electric brake) stay in the cab. Rail classes RVM_DBB_BR194_C and RVM_E94_088_C. Store id 4680420. Manual `schematicHeight` 540 (long checklists); drawing from the 3 Seenbahn Einsatzmerkblatt E 94 088. README `SOON 🟨`. Cab test must decide: whether the mod's held set keeps the wheel winding (mode switch) and what one lever push does, whether the reverser lock may stay pressed, and the brake bands.

- Thomas the Tank Engine (DTG, "Thomas & Friends Visit the West Somerset Railway", pak Artemis, 2026-10-12): capture `tools/captures/RVM_ART_Thomas_C.json` + `RVM_ART_Thomas_C_handle_probe.txt` (14:24 Bishops Lydeard–Minehead, blue Thomas layer; one footplate). SIMPLE-CONTROLS LOCO (`UsesSimpleControls` true, 21 nodes, an electric model underneath): no regulator, cut-off, brake valve, fireman controls, AWS or keys; the backhead is decoration. The only driving node `ThrottleControl` (SimpleThrottle, -1..1) is a SPRING handle: it reads 0 again 100 ms after the last API set, so it only acts while sets keep coming (dead zone ±0.25; 1 -> live 0.62..0.66 at 40 ms re-sends); holding + at a stand sets the reverser Forward and accelerates, holding - brakes and then reverses, with nothing sent the train coasts. `Reverser(Proxy)` -1 / 0 / 1 stores at a stand (any non-zero throttle set resets it) and follows the throttle sign while driving. Push buttons `Whislte` (sic, Horn), `Torch` (CabLights), `Headlights`. Doors are on the coaches (keys y / u used). Profile `tsw-thomas-dtg-thejag-1791773900` (`tools/mk/mkthomas.js`): ThrottleControl continuous on the left lever with the SNG geometry (power far, 0 at raw 0.5 ±0.05, brake nearest), ENG 1 = power 0.75 held / 0, ENG 2 = brake -1 held / 0 (fallback in case the app's hold re-sends), reverser [-1,0,1] inverted, Whistle right button, Headlights toggle on the round aux, Torch 0 / 1 on the mode switch; nothing else. KNOWN LIMITATION, for the user: the TCA lever only drives Thomas while it moves (the app re-sends on change only; `anti_jitter` silences a resting lever), so a held value or a re-send mode in the app would be needed for a resting lever to keep power on. Emergency 1, Independent 0 / 0.1..1), but a different class: `ReverserHandle` key, `DeadmansPedal`, `Horn` lever, `HeadlightSwitchProxy` Off 0 / Dim 0.33 / Bright 0.66, `SignalLightSwitch`, `SafetyControlCock` (WarningDevices). NEW GOTCHA, interlock lag: throttle, transition and reverser are checked against the other lever's state BEFORE its last change, so one set can leave a lever locked; a second set to a slightly different value of the lever just moved refreshes it (identical values do not). The profile `tsw-santafe-f7-dtg-thejag-1791833332` (`tools/mk/mksff7.js`, from mkf7.js) therefore gives the throttle and the transition lever TWO direct_control assignments on the same node, the notch values and the same notches +0.01, so every TCA change is sent twice (untested whether the app sends both in order). PARK BRK = ReverserHandle + GeneratorField + BrakeValveCutoutCock cut in (a spawned loco loads with all three off), gear lever DeadmansPedal, Horn lever, left aux SafetyControlCock, lights dial from HeadlightSwitchProxy + NumberLights + ClassLights. Store id 4679520. Slate scheme, Santa Fe logo, desk photo `tools/assets/santafe-f7-cab.jpg`; no drawing. README `SOON 🟨`. Cab test must decide: whether the doubled assignments free the lagging interlock. Santa Fe set complete.

- RUD TRIM = doors fleet-wide and the Class 31 reverser respaced (2026-10-11, after the user's first drive of the 31): the user wants the doors on RUD TRIM on every train, freight locos included (they run railtours), and prefers them over the parking brake. Eleven profiles changed to the door keys y / u: BR 155, BR 182, ES 64 U2 (pantograph and Hauptschalter now in the cab, as on the 101 / 185), BR 193 (AFB on / off and AFB confirm now in the cab, the AFB speed stays on the flap lever), Class 31 / 37 / 47 (handbrake in the cab), Class 66 / 86 / 87 / 90 (parking brake in the cab). Manuals re-rendered (RUD TRIM caption "Doors L / R", checklist lines), releases rebuilt, the `tools/mk/*.js` scripts updated to match (the 66 / 86 / 87 have no script); rule in CLAUDE.md. Class 31 reverser: the user found it flaky. An API probe (`node tools/probe_lever.js "Reverser_F (IrregularLever)"`) showed the game stores any value sent and switches notch at 0.18 / 0.5 / 0.84 with no gating (Off to Forward in one set works), so the uneven steps 0 / 0.2468 / 0.569 / 0.9243 over max 0.9243 gave uneven TCA bands (Off only about 13 % of the travel, Engine only 37 %). Now min 0 / max 1, steps 0 / 0.333 / 0.666 / 1 like the tested fleet reversers (equal quarter bands, the manual pills unchanged). The Class 37 (0.245 / 0.567 / 0.9225) and Class 47 (0.2505 / 0.5797 / 0.9428) reversers have the same uneven construction and were left alone per the one-train rule; respace them the same way if their cab tests show the same. README: the user drove the 31 again the same evening and was happy with the respaced reverser and the doors: `DONE ✅` (commit 73836dc).
- Class 31 cab change (2026-10-11, late evening): the user drove the 31 (reverser and doors fine, README briefly `DONE ✅`), then found that the TCA kept driving the F cab after a cab change. Cause: the profile was hard-wired to the `_F` nodes because `CurrentDrivableActor.Function.IS_GetActiveCab` answers "Node failed to return valid data" on this loco (as on the 43 / 66 / 86 / 87 / 90 / 220). Finding: the sibling call `CurrentDrivableActor.Function.GetActiveCab` works and follows the seat (polled every 3 s while the user walked: bFront true, then both false for about 25 s while walking, then bBack true), and the app resolves `{SIDE:F:B}` on this loco anyway (the B cab's reverser, brake valve and keys took the TCA's sets after the walk), so the app must fall back to the working call. Profile `tsw-class-31-dtg-thejag-1791708106` now uses `Reverser_{SIDE:F:B} (IrregularLever)`, `Horn_{SIDE:F:B}L (IrregularLever)` etc. on every cab-side node (reverser, both brakes, power controller, master key, brake key, horn, wipers, engine start / stop, AWS isolation; sander, DSD breaker, lights and handbrake are not cab-side), `tools/mk/mk31.js` has a `side()` helper for it. After a cab change the user flips PARK BRK down and up to key the new cab. README back to `SOON 🟨` until the user confirms the B cab on the TCA. Not yet rolled out to the other F-wired locos (43, 47, 37, 66, 86, 87, 90 and the German locos with `_F` nodes): the placeholder drops every command when both flags are false, so a loco whose `GetActiveCab` also fails would stop working entirely; test each one seated before switching it.
- Active-cab rollout (2026-10-11, late evening, user decision "switch them all now" after the 31's B cab worked on the TCA; Class 31 `DONE ✅`): new tool `tools/apply_side.js` rewrites every control node wired to the F cab whose B twin exists in the train's capture to the `{SIDE:F:B}` placeholder (`Reverser_F` → `Reverser_{SIDE:F:B}`, `Horn_FL` → `Horn_{SIDE:F:B}L`, `F_AutoBrake` → `{SIDE:F:B}_AutoBrake`, `BrakeKeyF` → `BrakeKey{SIDE:F:B}`), and the `tools/mk/*.js` scripts of those trains call it as their last step so a rerun keeps it. Applied to 22 profiles besides the 31: BR 101, 103, 110, 111, 112, 114, 140, 143, 146, 147, 155, 182, ES 64 U2, 185, 187, 193, Class 37, 47, 66, 86, 87, 90; releases rebuilt, manuals unchanged. Kept on F for lack of a B twin: BR 147 `SIFA_Switch_F (BackWall)` / `PZB_Switch_F (BackWall)`, Class 66 `F_FuseBox_DSDIsolate`. Left alone: BR 401 ICE 1 (its `_F` nodes have no `_B`, one cab per power car), Class 700 `SignalBell_F`, the HST (bare names). Untested on all 22: if a loco's `GetActiveCab` fails too, the placeholder drops every command and nothing moves at all; then put that train back on `_F` by hand (git history has the F-wired versions) and note it. Caveat for the Class 66: its B headlight lever has Day and Night swapped relative to F (capture note), so the lights dial will be off by one in the B cab. After a cab change the user flips PARK BRK down and up to key the new cab (the TCA only sends on a change).
- DB side views (2026-10-12, user: "You can use the DB side views"): the drawings from DB Notfallmanagement's Einsatzmerkblätter (rescue sheets, © DB AG; index on deutschebahn.com) are extracted by two Opus agents (pymupdf native raster, red arrows / labels / green fills removed, upscaled) into `tools/assets/<slug>-side.png`: 101, 110 (sheet 110/115/140, also used for the 140), 111, 112 = 114 = 143 (one sheet), 145 = 146 = 185 (one sheet), 147 = 187 (Bombardier sheet 05/2016), 155, 182 = ES 64 U2 (faint dotted source), 363 = 365 (V 60 sheet 05/2008), 294 (V 90 sheet 204–298), 194 (E 94 088, 3 Seenbahn); none for the 103 (photo only on its sheet) and the 193 (no sheet). The units (401, 403, 406, 411, 412, 422, 423, 425, 430, 440, 442, 612, 628, 642) were being prepared by the second agent. The drawings are wired through `tools/manual/diagrams.json` (keyed by the data file name, caption "Zeichnung: Deutsche Bahn AG, Einsatzmerkblatt BR xxx (mm/yyyy)"), which `render_manual.js` merges when a data file has no `diagram` of its own, so a rerun of a `tools/mk` script does not drop them. A lone loco at the fixed 48 px height is about 180 px wide (same scale as the unit drawings); if the user wants locos larger, add a `height` per entry in diagrams.json (the renderer honours `diagram.height`).
- DB BR 101 keys (2026-10-11, user cab test): the Richtungsschalter only moves with the reverser key in (`ReverserKeyF` / `ReverserKeyB`, MasterSwitch push button, In 1 / Out 0), which the profile did not set; PARK BRK set only the brake key. PARK BRK now sends the reverser key first and the brake key second (the ICE 1 / 401 pattern), both on `{SIDE:F:B}`; manual PARK BRK caption "Reverser key + brake key", first setup line adjusted; profile, release and manual rebuilt. The BR 146 has the same kind of unused `ReverserHandle_F` (MasterSwitch) beside an AFB-only PARK BRK, and the 155 keeps its `ReverserHandle_F` out on purpose (it locks the reverser at 1): if the 146's Richtungsschalter stays locked in the cab test, try adding its handle the 101 way. README: 101 stays `SOON 🟨`.
- DB BR 101 AFB lever (2026-10-11, user cab test "notch issue at 5 km/h"): probed `AFB_Speed_F` through the API with `AFB_F` switched on (the capture sweep ran with it off and saw nothing): sets below 0.05 store 0 (0 km/h), sets 0.05..0.105 snap to 0.1 = 5 km/h (the snap re-fired on every TCA reading, hence the clicking), from 0.11 the lever is continuous (0.11 = 10, 0.5 = 90, 1 = 200 km/h, in 5 km/h steps). Flap lever now banded like the 187: 0 km/h raw 1 ±0.04, 5 km/h 0.93 ±0.03 as its own step (0.1), speed zone 0.88..0.04, 200 at 0 ±0.04; manual flap pills 200 / … / 5 / 0 with the dot on 0. Driving with the AFB (user question): the AFB switch does not disable the Fahrschalter, it turns it into the traction limit; the loco only pulls while the AFB target is above the current speed, so set the speed on the flap lever first, then Fahrschalter towards Z. The user confirmed keys, cab change and AFB the same evening: `DONE ✅`.

## Next (handoff 2026-10-11 13:00, for a new session)

State: 45 trains are `SOON 🟨` awaiting the user's TCA cab test (34 from the overnight run 23:26–09:23, 11 from the second run 10:22–12:46: 422, 805, 807, 802 GBR, 387, 378, 700, 442, 185, 146). Everything is committed and pushed. The game was left in the BR 146 cab at Leipzig Hbf. Per-train details are in the entries above; each entry names its capture, profile, assumptions and what the cab test must decide.

1. **Spawn-mode trial: done 2026-10-11 13:12–13:50, it works, but the user decided the same afternoon to keep the timetable service loop as the default** (spawning looked awkward; cold keys and blind placement cost what the menus save). Spawn mode stays in `tools/CAPTURE_AGENT.md` as the secondary path for a one-lever re-capture of a known train or a train with no daytime service. The next train starts from the home screen by the service loop (Esc → Options → Main Menu first; the game sits in the 147 free-roam session). Trial findings: (a) Tab opens a radial in free roam (Spawn New Train / Respawn Last / Delete Train); the spawn list shows all owned stock on any route, UK units and tube stock included, with a search box. (b) There is no take-control entry anywhere; the only way is the "E Sit in Driver's Seat" prompt, which shows only with the crosshair on the seat cushion from the front (the first run lost 10 min aiming from behind). (c) Switch levers read on a spawned loco, and it comes up powered (pantograph up, screens lit, catenary at Engelsdorf), but its keys are cold: brake valve cut out, desk inactive, safety switches Isolated, which locked the 147's power side until Fable probed it. Home screen to cab: under 2 min; the whole 147 took 18 min against the 146's 20, most of it the 6.5 min sweep plus the interlock probe. The exact key sequence is in `tools/CAPTURE_AGENT.md` ("Spawn mode"). The game is still in that free-roam session in the 147 cab (parking brake applied by the probe).
